NFW Card Archive

NFW WRESTLEBOWL II (Night 2), 16 May 2007

16 May 2007
Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
WRESTLEBOWL II (Night 2) 16 May 2007 Dallas, Texas Introduction One dildo to screw them all One dildo to find them One dildo to ream them all And in the whorehouse, slime them One Down... Legacies should be the stories of heroes, but in this world...we employ equal opportunity. (FADEIN: The 2003 Draft Lottery for NFW Season 1 – joining in progress as EX-PRESIDENT QUENTIN SULLIVAN steps to the podium.) SULLIVAN: "Ok, OK, quiet down. What we are going to do, is get started with the lottery. Our two Conference Chairpersons will pick from the lottery bowl, until we are finished. We tossed up a quarter in the back, so starting first, as result of the coin toss, will be The NFW South, represented by Calvin Carlton." (Crowd murmurs and some boo as CARLTON steps up, sneering at anyone who he can see. Two hotel workers in tuxedos and white gloves wheel out a Plexiglas drum filled with slips of paper, and place it in front of the podium.) O’CONNOR: "And here we go, Woodman! The very first pick of NFW LOTTERY SEASON ONE!" H’WOOD: "Man, I can’t wait either. I hope you can note the dripping, bloody sarcasm in my voice." (CARLTON reaches down into the drum, and rolls his arm in there for a while… almost TOO long! SULLIVAN glares over at him in annoyance!) SULLIVAN: "The hell you doing in there?" CARLTON: "I don’t trust ANYBODY! I wanna make sure I get a great pick!" SULLIVAN: "Just pick already – don’t make this harder than it has to be for me." CARLTON: "Fine fine… (CARLTON finally decides on a paper, and pulls it out, eyes shut HARD! He brings the paper to his face and unfolds it, slowly opening his eyes… and they open wide! He runs and shoulders SULLIVAN out of the way and grabs the podium mic) GET OUTTA MY WAY! (Breathing heavily, to the point of hyperventilation) SULLIVAN: (Off-mic) "Who is it already!" CARLTON: "ICANDOTHIS! ICANDOTHIS! Ladies and gentlemen… OHMYGOD… OH MY GOD… Ladies and gentlemen… THERE IS A GOD…. My first pick… coming to NFW South… (Blows air out of his mouth and his eyes flutter) EVANAHO! EVANAHO! EVANAHO!" (CROWD EXPLODES! People in the back are slapping hands, and butting chests!) O’CONNOR: (V/O) "OH MY LORD! EVAN AHO IS IN NFW, AND HE’S GOING DOWN SOUTH!" H’WOOD: (V/O) "WHAT THE SH[BLEEEP!] EVAN AHO! WOW!" (The picture turns to black and white, and then fades out as a new scene replaces it...NFW All-Star Week: The War Games Cage Match…joined just before the explosion heard ‘round the world . . ) O’CONNOR: "Marx raking Rabesque’s face across the cage – he looks up at Stratton at shrugs, but the distraction gives Jean a chance to elbow the ‘Gentleman’ in the ribs! ‘Star – can you tell what Stratton is doing?" WILDSTAR: "He’s taping those things wrapped in cardboard to the joints of the cage…and they’re all connected with something, I can’t make out. It looks like there’s something wrapped in plastic within the cardboard…" O’CONNOR: "I don’t like the smell of this. (crowd cheers!) And lookit this – Hornet has turned Manson over into a Boston Crab! Stratton has reached the top of the cage and Shizaki is yelling at him, now!" WILDSTAR: "Something isn’t right, Kerry – Stratton and Riddick have some weird look in their eye…Stratton is now staddling the top of the cage and taping those things all along two top sides of the cage…" O’CONNOR: "Shizaki staggering up and right into a Shawn Matthews sleeper hold! Rabesque trying to suplex Marx – NO! Marx reverses the hold and suplexes the Canadian into the other ring! Marx turning ar—" WILDSTAR: "Stratton is telling Riddick to run away – what the hell is going on? He just climbed down the cage like Spider-Man now he’s running away! Whoa – there’s sparks up there! O’CONNOR: "STRATTON LIT SOMETHING UP THERE! Lindsay Troy is JUM—" (SFX: BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOM!!!!!!!!!!) (CUTTO: A HUGE EXPLOSION followed by smaller sets of explosions shockwave around the whole cage to crowd screams, before the whole structure is under a THICK haze of smoke!) WILDSTAR: "HOLY SHIT!" O’CONNOR: "OH MY GOD! THE CAGE EXPLODED!" (CUTTO: Fans at RINGSIDE panicked and shouting at other fans to move back! CUTTO: LINDSAY TROY army-crawling away from the ringside area to the aisleway…) O’CONNOR: "ITS FALLING OVERRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRR!" (SFX: METAL SHREDDING IN HALF! CROWD: "OHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!") WILDSTAR: "I—I don’t believe this…" O’CONNOR: "HALF THE CAGE JUST TOPPLED OVER! Everyone in the left side of the ring, including the referee just had half the cage FALL ON THEM! NOBODY is moving!" (CUTTO: AVERY PROSSER walking out from the curtains clapping with a Luciferian smile, the rest of the SEVEN DEADLY SINS in uniform standing behind him with stoic faces as NEIL RIDDICK runs up to PROSSER jumping up and down celebrating – the crowd throwing trash down near the entrance where they all stand triumphantly... that scene goes through a FADEOUT... The scene stays black for a moment... first the sounds of a crowd in hysterics is heard, and the screen does a slow FADEIN: Wrestlebowl One Ultratitle Finals – MANSON vs. SOUTHERN...) SIMS: (V/O) "FIFTEEN SECONDS! FIFTEEN SECONDS ARE REMAINING!" O'CONNOR: "This crowd has absolutely LOST it! Southern drags Manson to the center of the ring near Herpin!" SIMS: (V/O) "TEN SECONDS REMAINING! TENNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNN! NIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIINE!" O'CONNOR: "SOUTHERN COVERS FOR THE PIN! ("EIGHHHHHHHHT!") HERPIN'S CRAWLING OVER! HOW CAN HE EVEN SEE!?!?" H'WOOD: "I don't know, I can't even watch right now! ("FIIIIIIIVE!") Tell me when it's over..." O'CONNOR: "HERPIN IS STARTING TO COUNT! ONEEEEEEEEEE! ("THREEEEEEE! TWOOOOOOO!") TWOOOOOOOOOOO! SOUTHERN'S SCREAMING TO COUNT FASTER! ("ONNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNE!") THRRRRRRRRREEEEEE----" RING! DING! RING-A-LING-DING! DING! DING! H'WOOD: "WHAT HAPPENED!?!?!? WHAT HAPPENED!?!!?!?" O'CONNOR: "I DON'T KNOW!" O'CONNOR: "It looked like Herpin counted to three, but I don't know if it before the bell started ringing, I really don't!" (CUTTO: QUENTIN SULLIVAN and other officials inside the ring, talking to HERPIN who’s having a hard time standing up against the turnbuckles. HERPIN looks like he’s explaining things, pointing to his eyes and head as he talks...finally SULLIVAN walks up to LEE-BABY SIMS and whispers in his ear. Meanwhile, MANSON is still nearly unconscious on the mat – still lying on his back as SHANE SOUTHERN is on his knees, his hands on his hips. His face cut open, he can only watch in disbelief at what’s going on...) O’CONNOR: "It looks like we’ve gotten a mutual decision inside the ring between the officials and the President and Lee-Baby Sims is about to talk..." DING! DING! DING! SIMS: "At the time of THIRTY MINUTES, the results of this match are MICHAEL MANSON with TWO FALLS...SHANE SOUTHERN with...ONE FALL...." (SOUTHERN’s head drops, the crowd CANNOT believe it letting out a VERY LOUD reaction of disbelief....) SIMS: "THEREFORE.....THE WINNER OF THIS MATCH! BY TWO FALLS TO ONE FALL!" (MUSICUP: ‘Mechanical Animals’ by Marilyn Manson) SIMS: "AND THE WINNER OF NEW FRONTIER WRESTLING’S INAUGURAL ULTRATITLE SEASON! AND STILLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL NFW WORLD HEAVYWEIGHT CHAAAAAMMMMMMMPION! MIIIIIIIIIIIIIICHAEL! MANNNNNNNNNNNSUHHHHHHHNNNNNNNNN!" (FADETOBLACK) KERRY O’CONNOR: (V/O) "In the inaugural season of the New Frontier Wrestling, the federation brought a fresh, new concept to the scene of American wrestling. Ratings shot up, sold out shows in the Mid-South, Upper East Coast and Vegas only propelled the federation closer to domination. By the beginning of 2005, not only had the promotion changed the landscape of the sport in the fans’ minds, but it had also developed into one of the most revered squared circles for wrestlers to compete in. Michael Manson and Shane Southern, two men that had served as ambassadors for this federation met in the finals and made history. Michael Manson united the NFW World Championship and Ultratitle Season 1 Championship for one night, only for Shane Southern to break it across his back... completing a turn to the dark side that had manifested itself over two years... " (MONTAGE: MANSON rolling out his tank in Week 9, forming the East Conference ... EDDIE MAYFIELD unmasking as WASP at Crashmas 2004 ... CRAIG MILES, ELI FLAIR, TROY WINDHAM and MAYFIELD brawling at Wrestlebowl 1...) O’CONNOR: (V/O) "Along the way, the New Frontier kept expanding ... from the Mid-South, Philadelphia, Boston and all the way to Vegas, it had conquered America... and Michael Manson’s newly developed Eastern Conference of freaks, geeks and drug-addicted nymphomaniacs started getting an underground following, not only here ... but elsewhere as well." (MONTAGE: Manson and NOT Jonathan Marx fighting in Dublin, Ireland... Manson unmasking him as the real Marx in New York City . . Manson and Bloodhunt brawling in front of a Star Wars cosplay studio audience in San Rafael, California...home of Skywalker Ranch.) O’CONNOR: (V/O) "And so the season ended with Michael Manson looming as the champion, but a war on the forefront of Season 2. With the attraction of the greatest talent in the ring, the most extraordinary production and thematic concepts in the presentation – superstars, World Champions of other promotions filed in and enlisted for this war..." (MONTAGE: The Season 2 Preseason – EDDIE MAYFIELD and CRAIG MILES brawling. TROY WINDHAM being coronated as UWA World Champion by AVERY PROSSER. TOM ADLER and TROY WINDHAM battling in the infamous lumberjacks match...) O’CONNOR: (V/O) "Ten years of feuds, wounds and politics came down in a crushing manner as Eddie Mayfield brought back the black flag of the UWA to try and take Craig Miles out of this sport and destroy the promotion of NFW. In the molds of the old gunslingers, Miles used his power to expand the situation into the Western Frontier..." (MONTAGE: ROOK BLACK slicing STEVE CHRIST’s tongue with barbed wire... MARCUS CAMERON powerbombing DEACON...ELI FLAIR lassoing JOEY MELTON ... KVC hitting the Creedzillinator on SEYMOUR ALMASY...LINDSAY TROY flying off the Hulk-A-Thon cage...) O’CONNOR: (V/O) "And as the war in the West raged on, the Eastern Conference took New Frontier past the national scene and into the heart of the orient... into one of the most revered areas for wrestling GLOBALLY. Opening the doors at FZM Arena in Tokyo, Japan... Michael Manson, Jonathan Marx, Dan Ryan, Lindsay Troy would see legends, superstars and ambitious young stalwarts enter their conference in hopes of becoming the Season 2 Ultratitle Champion, but also the holder of the World Title around Manson’s waist...what started as something honorable turned into something extreme, bloody and downright insane – just like its Western Counterpart." (MONTAGE: DOC SILVER winning the World Title...FELIX RED and DAN RYAN in their infamous first matchup... YORI YAKAMO JUNIOR headbanging while thousands of fans shake their glow-in-the-dark dildoes in the air... BEAU MICHAELS hugging KOOTER CRUISE...JONATHAN MARX holding his head and talking to himself... KIN HIROSHI getting his chest pumped by paramedics...) O’CONNOR: (V/O) "Last night, New Frontier Wrestling took one step closer towards its future of global dominance in this industry. This two-night Pay-Per-View would close out NFW’s singular grasp on the Ultratitle and propel to a global event broadcasted by Extreme Sports Entertainment Television, the television station built on the backbone of NFW...and displaying this PAY-PER-VIEW you’ve ordered. Two marquee matches would put a war to behind in Craig Miles and Eddie Mayfield’s final match with each other, and possibly ever for their careers... and Kin Hiroshi and Felix Red settling their season-long feud with the NFW World Championship at stake." (FADETOBLACK...) ... One to Go. (MUSICUP: 'All Down the Line' -- Rolling Stones) "Yeah, heard the diesel drumming all down the line. " (FADEIN: FELIX RED charging up the ramp during the main event World Championship match from last night! He launches into the air, but somehow KIN HIROSHI catches him and drives him out of the ring and through a nearby table with his uber-destructive Japanese Burning Hammer!) "Oh, heard the wires a humming all down the line." (SUPER SLO-MO REPLAY: The referee counting to ten, signaling for the bell and HIROSHI falling to his knees... ) "Oh, hear the children crying all down the line." (FADEOUT as KIN HIROSHI cradling the NFW World Championship like a newborn baby, fireworks exploding over his head. His eyes are welling up in tears as the shock of victory is overwhelming...) "We'll be watching out for trouble, yeah." (FADEIN: EDDIE MAYFIELD and CRAIG MILES – brawling up the ramp...it starts rolling into a montage of them brawling through the crowd! Craig suicide diving off the balcony! Eddie bullrushing Craig stuffed in a cart towards the edge of the ramp!) "And we'd better keep the motor running, yeah." (MONTAGE cnt’d: MILES stalking MAYFIELD with the hockey mask covering his face, fireaxe clutched against his chest! Eddie with a bloody chiclets smile for a nearby camera ...Eddie hitting the Sunset Flip Powerbomb onto a box of thumbtacks!) "Well, you can't say yes and you can't say no," (MONTAGE cnt’d: Flashing clips of both men refusing to quit – Eddie locking in the FPS, Craig locking in the Cockring! Eddie dropping Craig with the Screwjob 2 through a table! Craig hitting the Top Rope Flaming Chair Elbow onto Eddie who’s bungee strapped to a table!) "Just be right there when the whistle blows." (CLOSEUP: MILES and MAYFIELD... hugging in the middle of the ring, POISON IVY and the world watching in shock.) "I need a sanctified girl with a sanctified mind to help me now." (FADEOUT as THE PROFESSIONALS deliver a Hart Attack to Nixon, knocking her off the ramp and into the crowd... who start crowd-surfing her towards the exit.) "Yeah, all the people singing all down the line." (FADEIN: LIVE! Dallas, Texas – Reunion Arena. Pyrotechnics explode everywhere, fireworks shooting to the ceiling, sparklers propelling to the ceiling from the rinposts and raining down in multiple colors. CUTTO: The cameras swoop around the 25,000 fans ROARING as the FRONTIERtron and SMOKEscreens start revolving through another montage as the song continues...ELI FLAIR and DAN RYAN’s reel off as split-screens show highlights clips of their mic spot and RYAN powerbombing BEAU MICHAELS, attacking KOOTER CRUISE and then getting dragged out of the arena in handcuffs by security.) "We're gonna bust another bottle, yeah." (MONTAGE: MICHAEL MANSON defeating SHANE SOUTHERN at Wrestlebowl 1, MANSON losing the World Title to DOC SILVER in the early parts of this season, MICHAEL ALEXANDER PAUL MANSON stomping grapes as JOEY MELTON watches while smoking a cigar and laughing...) "All down the line." (CUTTO: REUNION ARENA fans jumping over each other as cameras get a close-up. A couple of fans are wearing brand spanking new "MILES/MAYFIELD 3: Two hours of BULLSH*T, and all I got was this crappy t-shirt." QUICK CUTTO: Random signs – "Say No to the Pros!" "THANK YOU MICHAEL MANSON – Now I know the true meaning of Jesus!" "ELI FLAIR – EXTREME to the BLOODY end") "I need a shot of salvation, baby, once in a while." (MONTAGE: LINDSAY TROY getting beat down at HULK-A-THON by Vic Creed! TROY and CREED mouthing off to each other at the Western Semifinals! CREED destroying MILES in Week 5! CREED stretchering ALMASY at Wrestlestock! TROY eliminating RYAN and MARX at Hulk-A-Thon! TROY fighting off ELI FLAIR as JOEY MELTON tries to help her…) "Hear the whistle blowing, hear it for a thousand miles." (MONTAGE: JASON PAYNE turning beet-red, while screaming into a microphone! KIN HIROSHI holding the World Championship in his hands, crying...MITTENS T. CAT, CHUCK WOOLERY and DON KING posing like they’re in CHARLIE’S ANGELS...) "We're gonna open up the throttle, yeah." (CUTTO: Fans shaking their glow-in-the-dark dildo’s in the balcony area, where a banner reading "YORI’S LOVE PALCE" hangs down, waving like a flag! "Just be right there when the whistle blows." (CUTTO: Back LIVE! More explosions, more pyrotechnics! BACKSTAGE: ELI FLAIR taping his fists, MIKE RANDALLS leading NOVA and YORI YAKAMO JUNIOR in a prayer in a smoke-filled room littered with sleeping hookers, a lot of drugs and several guitars – NFW SECURITY watching them nervously...DAN RYAN sneaking along a back alleyway in the darkness... KIN HIROSHI alone his locker room, staring at his championship in awe...) "I need a sanctified mind to help me out right now." (MONTAGE: NOVA reaching for the Golden Ticket in the Western Conference finals from a twenty-five foot ladder, but ELI FLAIR comes flying into the picture...YORI literally getting the shit KICKED out of him by FELIX RED... NOVA on the mat, the golden ticket in his hands... YORI puffing his cheeks out, running against the ropes and hitting the HULK LEGDROP ... the screens split everywhere, showing their victory celebrations as the cameras swoop up, down, around and all over the ground before reaching KERRY O’CONNOR and LAMONT HOLLYWOOD – the fans roaring! The music cuts, the crowd volume is amped as O’CONNOR adjusts his forest green suit and brown khakis ... H’WOOD looks on nervously, sporting the all-white route with a pearl ascot, sportscoat, slacks and shoes.) Welcome to the Jungle O’CONNOR: "Welllllllllcome to WRESTLEBOWL NIGHT TWO with NEW FRONTIER WRESTLING, the home of overblown introductions renowned worldwide! And the world, the industry... everyone is still in shock over just last night’s shocks, twists, surprises and of course, REUNIONS." H’WOOD: "I’d just like to say that anything I’ve said about Craig Miles in the past three or four years, has been completely in jest and I hope he knows that..." O’CONNOR: "We might as well paint a yellow streak down your back, Lamont! And if either of us aren’t employed after this season, I’m definitely blaming you. Yes, the PROFESSIONALS showed THEIR true colors, last night ... after two hours of nearly killing each other, the culmination of a three-year war ended with nothing, but deceit and LIES. Within moments, of the 100th minute of the I Quit match... they embraced, and then brutally attacked Nixon Mil—well, I don’t even know who last name anymore!" H’WOOD: "They played the world for fools, they played the WHOLE core of the Western Conference for idiots. Thank god, I knew it from the beginning ‘cause Craig Miles pulled me aside and told me everything before it beg—" O’CONNOR: "Oh brother." H’WOOD: "What?" O’CONNOR: (rolling his eyes) "All I know now is that the woman we THOUGHT Craig Miles was married to... was nothing, but an NFW employee that apparently carried the aborted child of Troy Windham. We do know that the Professionals are in Miles’ skybox this evening, and I’m sure there’s going to be a few people that would LOVE to visit them. And no matter what you say, I REFUSE to believe that you knew about this from the start – as its apparently been planned... possibly before NFW even opened! We’re going to get answers tonight, and maybe sweet justice for yourself after running down this federation for the UWA ...and the man that’s still our boss, Craig Miles as well." (H’WOOD nervously gulps and looks to the camera with a puppy-dog face...) H’WOOD: "I swear Craig... buddy, I love this place! I love my job! And I love you big guy!" O’CONNOR: "Put a sock in it, Woodman – your job was gone a long time ago, if that’s their decision...and let’s move on from that situation to the one that ended a lot more satisfyingly for myself. Kin Hiroshi DEFEATING Felix Red in the main event (crowd cheers!) in a last man’s standing match to win the NFW World Heavyweight Championship, which he will defend tonight against the Mittens T. Cat and Jason Payne! I’ve watched the trials and tribulations of Hiroshi for a LONG time... and throughout the ordeal, he remained loyal to NFW as well as its fans ... and to see him take down Felix Red after Felix nearly ended his career was AMAZING." H’WOOD: "I’ve got two words for you, Beanfry – RICK RYCONIK. (boos!) The man who took the title off of Ricky Zane in 2001, only to get it snatched away in seconds by Armando Montezuma. Kin Hiroshi hit the highest of highs last night, which is pretty amazing considering his lifestyle (O’CONNOR groans), but that dream can turn into a nightmare tonight. Jason Payne, Mittens T. Cat – you’ve got two ends of a spectrum coming at you, and Hiroshi doesn’t even have to be pinned to lose the title he nearly sacrificed his life to win!" O’CONNOR: "Along with his title defense, there is so much we’re going to see tonight. The final matches of TWO wrestlers that have done so much for this sport over the last decade, in TWO matches that pretty much defines their careers in so many ways. ‘The King of Extreme’ Eli Flair (LOUD cheers! ELI chants...) he was SUPPOSED to face Dan Ryan tonight in a street fight, but Ryan’s been BANNED from the arena and possibly SUSPENDED for his actions last night." H’WOOD: "Well, Eli should be thankful he hasn’t left this place on a stretcher, or with a broken neck. Lord knows he’s tested that in all kinds of matches that those creaky knees never should’ve walked into..." O’CONNOR: "Well, knowing this place... we may be in for more than few surprises. And the other man leaving the sport after tonight is MICHAEL MANSON. (LOUD OVATION!) The winner of the Ultratitle of Season One, as well as a former holder of the World Heavyweight Championship. He’s been Commissioner, he’s ruled the East, he’s brought us manic matches, monkeys and near-riots all over the world. Tonight, in the first ever VINEYARD match, Michael Manson ends his career against the first-ever Ultratitle champion, Joey Melton! Two former Ultratitle winners, two LEGENDS in this sport settling a feud that’s beyond my comprehension..." H’WOOD: "Well, you don’t know fine wines, or the perils of making them Beanfry. The Napa Valley is really just an Indian graveyard that voodoo practitioners maintain – Manson told me at the lunch buffet this afternoon." O’CONNOR: "Riiiiiiiight. Two other matches could shape the future of this federation greatly, as in a grudge match, Lindsay Troy and Kodiak Vic Creed will throw down one more time. We’ve seen if often over the last three years, and we’re going to see it again – both have vowed to end this through any means possible. And lord knows the winner might be in line for title shots and opportunities..." H’WOOD: "I think Lindsay Troy might be smoking some of the drugs around here a little too much, if she thinks she’s walking out of this match. Creed’s a monster, and if she’s lucky, he won’t split open her thighs like a coconut... finally getting NFW’s censors to act." O’CONNOR: "I don’t think we have censors, Lamont. ‘Cause if we did, I don’t think half of the stuff we saw on ESEN over the last few weeks would’ve been aired – especially with Joe the Plumber, one of the participants in tonight’s Television Title battle royal... we don’t know ALL of the participants, but superstars such as Maggot and Shawn Hart have signed up and are in the building tonight." H’WOOD: "Leave it to Miles to find freaks in the Jersey plumber’s union to show up in Texas with a (BLEEP!)it covered plunger...wait, I mean that’s a great idea! What a wonderful way to end the show!" O’CONNOR: "... um, Woodman..." H’WOOD: "What?" O’CONNOR: "NOVA AND YORI FOR THE ULTRATITLE CHAMPIONSHIP!" H’WOOD: "Oh, yeah right...well, that’s important too." O’CONNOR: "You’re damn right it is! Those two wrestlers haven’t just been through hell..." H’WOOD: "That’s right, they’ve eaten a sh(BLEEP!)load of drugs and made some wacky, wacky films on camera." O’CONNOR: "Oh brother. Well, they’re going somewhere else tonight... a first ever match of its sort... and well, its aptly titled the "F(BLEEP!)k your own Adventure" match – a wheel that will spin with ten stipulations, every fifteen minutes until one of those two either survive, or actually win the second season Ultratitle." H’WOOD: "And honestly, I don’t think anyone knows how to bet on the ending of that one. Something that’s been destined for weeks, the implosion of the world as time and space bend into multiple vortexes only to see those two most likely destroy its fabrics and our reality. Nothing like the end of existence, Beanfry... (H’WOOD smiles at the camera) GREAT IDEA CRAIG! (thumbs up) I’m really looking forward to it." O’CONNOR: "We’re all treading on different ground after last night, and I’m pretty sure the Woodman’s right when saying we’re not going to be the same after TONIGHT." H’WOOD: "It’s a brave new world, Beanfry... and there’s not enough acid anywhere to understand it." O’CONNOR: "From the Doors of Jim Morrison to the Doors of Perception, we’re taking a journey tonight! But first ... we’re going to get set up for our opening match of the night, and head backstage with the man we like to call ‘THE MOLE’ cause he gets the inside scoop on things…Richard Reese is standing by with the World Champion, Kin Hiroshi! Richard – welcome to NFW!" (CUTTO: The backstage locker room where KIN HIROSHI is sitting on a bench ...wearing the same attire as last night’s show, blood streaked everywhere on his ring pants and boots. Lying across his lap, glittering off the lights is the NFW World Heavyweight Championship. HIROSHI’s fingers gently touch the front plate, his eyes wide as saucers. Standing in front of him and facing the cameras is RICHARD ‘SMITTY’ REESE – a balding man with black hair in his mid 30’s, wearing a tucked in royal blue Nordstrom button-down shirt, Dockers Khakis and thin-rimmed black glasses.) REESE: "Thanks Kerry, this is RSR coming right at ya!" H’WOOD: (V/O) "Yeah, thanks SMITTY." (REESE gets an annoyed look on his face...) REESE: "I told you to stop calling me that, Lamont." H’WOOD: (V/O) "Get used to it, kid." REESE: (rolls his eyes) "Whatever. Knowing I would be starting for New Frontier Wrestling after this season, something I’m sure Craig Miles hasn’t told Lamont Hollywood yet...I arrived early this morning to take a tour of the premises, checking out the backstage locker room where I happened to see this man sitting right here, the NFW World Heavyweight Champion, Kin Hiroshi." O’CONNOR: (V/O) "Are you telling us that Kin might’ve not left the arena since last night!?!?" REESE: "Looks to be that way Kerry, I did alert medical personnel as well as Mister Miles, but everyone seems to be of opinion that this par for the course mentally for not only Kin, but this place as a whole. Your fame and riches were everything No matter what the cost (MONTAGE: TOM ADLER and VIC CREED getting pinned by NOVA in the Conference Semifinals, FELIX RED failing to stand up on the first night of this pay-per-view’s telecast, YORI YAKAMO JUNIOR missing the Legdrop on NOVA...the picture fades to black.) Now you wish you hadn't blown it (The guitar solo starts in the background as EDDIE MAYFIELD and CRAIG MILES stand across the ring, the crowd besides themselves... and all of a sudden they HUG!) Well lay it all on me (MONTAGE: NOVA falling into the HYPERSPACE KID’s arms with ULTRATITLE trophy glittering off the flashing lights, ELI FLAIR and POISON IVY after his match with DAN RYAN, KIN HIROSHI and the World Championship alone together backstage...) Lay it all on me (FAST-FORWARD MONTAGE: NATHAN STORM pushing CRAIG MILES off the ladder on the ramp tonight, DAN RYAN powerbombing BEAU MICHAELS through a table last night, FRANKIE FARGO and CAMERON CRUISE brawling side by side with CORAL AVALON and a SILVER triplet. POWERMASTER and MANPOWER, POWERMASTER rocket-launching YORI at Hulk-A-Thon onto LINDSAY TROY, MICHAEL ALEXANDER PAUL MANSON turning his night lamp off after kissing a framed picture of HORNET gracing the cover of Forbes Magazine.) Lay it all on me (LUDRICOUS SPEED MONTAGE: SAM BECKETT saying "Oh Boy" as he looks at SMITTY naked for the first time, NOVA slackjawed as THE CHAIRMAN huffs down an entire burrito joint himself, MIKE RANDALLS and ROBOYORI doing a stereo robot, MITTENS T. CAT and the SAN DIEGO CHICKEN fighting on a scaffold. JONATHAN MARX talking to himself, SCOTT RIKTOR laughing in a bear suit, VIC CREED backhanding a hooker against the wall, MIKE RANDALLS lifting off for the Discomforting Elbow Drop Around the World at Wrestlestock...SCOTT RIKTOR spearing ALIAS through a barbed wire door! Lay it on me (CNT’D: DAN RYAN looking out the cockpit at his plane crashing, ELI FLAIR hanging off a running horse, punching at JOEY MELTON, ELI FLAIR holding onto a speeding monster truck punching at NOVA. FELIX RED putting KIN in the Felix Stretch, FELIX RED hitting the Swanton bomb on DOC SILVER, CRAIG MILES and JOEY MELTON jumping out of the way of the O.S.S. on the General Lee Jetskis, CALVIN CARLTON honking the horn! Lay it on me (CNT’D: ELI FLAIR getting TROY WINDHAM to tap! CREEDZILLINATOR on SEYMOUR ALMASY at Wrestlestock! MANSON-BOT vs. YORI-BOT! CRAIG MILES drinking his own blood, TROY WINDHAM unzipping for JOEY MELTON, NOVA covering YORI for the pin... FADETOBLACK with the crowd counting...) Come on down baby Ease your worried mind ... ... "I can’t believe I lost..." "You wanted to melt the damn thing into a dildo, Yori." "So?" (FADEIN: The final lyrics echo lightly in the background from the montage video for a moment. Meanwhile, a rising quarter-moon becomes the focus above a Badlands campsite, glowing from a large bonfire. There are two medieval lord-sized tents that are drawn shut, but candles flickering inside. The trot of a horse is heard and STRIDER, the white stallion of MIKE RANDALLS enters the scene. RANDALLS is atop her, smoking a rolled cigarette who he hands to a scarred, tattered and disheveled YORI YAKAMO JUNIOR.) YORI: "Where are we, Mike? I’d really like to put on my robe and slippers, pop in my Madam’s Family Values anal gonzo f—" RANDALLS: "You are somewhere safe, with people who love you. I am sorry you have not fulfilled your quest for the Ultratitle." (RANDALLS helps YORI dismount... as he does, he hears PLEASANT shrieks for the first time, both men are no doubt glad they do not sound the same as the Oranguon-kai, the spider-monkey/orangutan zombie hybrid beasts in Ape Hitler’s army. Instead, five perky 5’5, 105 pound women with perfect C-Cups start back flipping out of the tents, dressed in cheerleader’s uniforms with big "Y’s" on the front, and the silouhuette of the YOR-ABBIT on the back.) YORI: "BRANDI! MANDI! CANDI! BAMBI! (fixing his thin mustache bar) LEIIILANIIIIII!" (The girls leap onto YORI, knocking him on the ground, it is a brunette, a blonde, a red-head, a dirty blonde and an Asian quintuplet known as the CRIME-FIGHTING CHEERLEADERS! Suddenly, YORI beams with a bright blue light as if a spirit took control. He looks around slightly confused at the cheerleaders.) SAM: "Oh boy." (The girls look at him sadly, petting him on the cheeks and hair...) MANDI: "Awwwwwwww, Yori doesn’t remember Mike..." RANDALLS: "He will." YORI: (looking around confused) "Hey Mike...you haven’t seen ROBOYORI anywhere? Have you?" (RANDALLS bows his head, as do the girls... except LEILANI who cuddles up next to YORI.) LEILANI: "That’s why we’re here, Yori..." RANDALLS: "RoboYori is lost to the shadow, the Crime-Fighting Cheerleaders and myself have pledged ourselves in helping you finding him." (RANDALLS bows to one knee, as do the girls.) YORI: "I get to have sex with the cheerleaders, right?" (The girls giggle, jumping on YORI as RANDALLS laughs heartedly at his good-natured companions... and so the scene fades backwards, away from the desert... away from Earth and into the luminous belt of Orion, swaying past the Milky Way and out of our galaxy. Past the stars, past time...the scene becoming darker and darker...) "KOOTER! Its time to lock up for the night!" (And finally, we find ourselves looking at the back of KOOTER CRUISE’s head as he stares bowling-sized black crystal ball... RANDALLS, YORI and the cheerleaders in his view. As the picture peels back, KOOTER CRUISE is standing on the stoop of trailer in the middle of a desert. Three orange/red suns are setting, KOOTER’s with the ponytail and samurai’s robe donned...Behind him at the door, MIKE RANDALLS looking ten years older with a graying beard watches KOOTER for a moment, smiling before he heads inside the trailer. KOOTER sheds a single tear on the crystal ball, the scene fading out of it...as he places it down next to a similar-sized ball that’s a different color, seven of them in all.) RANDALLS: "It is time Kooter, for ALL of us." (KOOTER turns around and faces the camera, walking towards it until he fills up the screen, he raises his hands... more tears streaming out of his eyes as he claps 3 times...) FADE TO BLACK.
